Yet a central normative question in this domain has remained underexplored: who has the final authority to determine the meaning of one's own emotion? This study addresses the question from the epistemological side of measurement's structural limits. We define a meaning distribution as the distribution of labels assigned by annotators drawn from a population under a fixed annotation protocol, and decompose its uncertainty into reducible and irreducible components. We then demonstrate that, while emotion AI can assign high-confidence point labels and discriminate real differences at an aggregate level, the irreducible component of the meaning distribution for individual instances cannot be estimated with adequate coverage under realistic annotator counts, a systematic divergence we term the epistemic gap. The key finding is that high device confidence does not constitute evidence that irrecoverable meaning has been recovered. From this epistemic gap, together with an explicitly stated normative premise, namely that the output of a system which cannot recover a quantity in principle must not be treated as its authoritative determination, we derive the norm that the final interpretive authority over the meaning of one's emotion is procedurally reserved for the experiencing subject, the norm of affective sovereignty. These results suggest that the design, evaluation, and regulation of emotion AI should place explicit allocation of interpretive authority, rather than accuracy maximisation, at their core.

</details>


### [253] [CSTrader: A Testbed for Language-Grounded Trading in a Community-Driven Virtual Asset Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31461)
*Yao Shi,Kingfung Luo,Nan Tang,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CSTrader多智能体框架，利用大语言模型将非结构化文本转化为CS2皮肤市场的交易决策，在极度波动的市场环境中实现了超越基准的7.58%累计收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利基资产市场（如CS2皮肤市场）具有规模小、波动大、受社区讨论和平台规则驱动等特点，传统量化模型难以建模，但为研究LLM如何将非结构化文本转化为交易行为提供了理想的试验场。

Method: 构建CSTrader多智能体框架：首先整合多种异构信号源，然后由专门智能体分别进行技术分析、流动性分析、事件分析和（反向）情绪分析，最后通过风险控制、交易摩擦和投资组合管理智能体在真实交易摩擦下生成买入、卖出或持有决策。

Result: 在高度波动时期的真实CS2数据上，CSTrader在多个LLM骨干下均持续优于下跌的市场指数（-15.62%）和简单的单提示LLM基线，最高实现7.58%的累计收益且风险可控。消融实验表明流动性、反向情绪和交易摩擦智能体对将噪声语言信号转化为稳定收益至关重要。

Conclusion: 利基、语言驱动的市场可作为未来语言到行动研究的有用基准，CSTrader框架验证了多智能体协作在处理嘈杂文本信号进行交易决策中的有效性。

Abstract: Niche asset markets, such as Counter-Strike 2 (CS2) weapon skins, are small, volatile, and heavily driven by community discussions and platform rules. These properties make them hard for traditional quantitative models, but provide an ideal testbed for studying how large language models (LLMs) turn unstructured text into trading actions. We present CSTrader, a multi-agent framework for language-grounded trading in the CS2 skin market. The system first integrates heterogeneous signals from various sources, then uses specialized agents for technical analysis, liquidity, events, and (reversed) sentiment, and finally applies risk control, transaction friction, and portfolio management agents to produce buy, sell, or hold decisions under realistic trading frictions. We build a live-like evaluation environment with real CS2 data from a highly volatile period and evaluate several recent LLM backbones. Across models, CSTrader consistently outperforms both a falling market index (-15.62%) and simple single-prompt LLM baselines, achieving up to a 7.58% cumulative return with controlled risk. Ablation studies show that liquidity, reversed sentiment, and transaction friction agents are crucial for turning noisy language signals into stable profits, suggesting that niche, language-driven markets are a useful benchmark for future language-to-action research. Code is available at: https://github.com/IatomicreactorI/CSGOTrading?tab=readme-ov-file#quick-start

</details>


### [254] [CLOUDADV: Decision-Aligned Instance Sizing with Zero-Shot Foundation Models under Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31470)
*Jack Bell,Giacomo Carfi,Gerlando Gramaglia,Andrea Simioni,Daniele Fontani,Vincenzo Lomonaco*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CLOUDADV是一个面向工程师的交互式咨询系统，用于工作负载漂移下的云实例规格调整。它结合零 shot 时间序列预测与有界推荐生成，在日、周、月三个规划周期上提供建议。使用大语言模型离线生成参考推荐，小模型在线部署。在7个生产VM案例中，推荐将月成本从约1503美元降至708美元（节省52.9%），最高超标率1.5%。结果表明零 shot 基础模型可支持非平稳云环境中的决策对齐配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云虚拟机常被过度配置，造成可避免的成本和运营低效。现有方法需要为每个租户重复训练、验证和部署模型，运营负担重。需要一种能处理非平稳工作负载漂移、同时降低运营开销的实例规格调整方案。

Method: CLOUDADV系统结合零 shot 时间序列预测（使用Chronos-2）与有界推荐生成，覆盖日、周、月三个规划周期。利用大语言模型（更高容量模型离线生成参考推荐，小模型在线评估对齐）构建结构化决策上下文，包括历史利用率、预测摘要、VM元数据、候选实例、定价和启发式规则。评估使用滚动起点预测精度和模拟Azure成本节省及超标率。

Result: 在7个生产VM案例中，参考推荐将月成本从约1503美元降至708美元，节省795美元/月（52.9%），最高超标率1.5%。Chronos-2虽未最小化每个预测指标，但常诱导出与有监督每VM基线相似的推荐模式。

Conclusion: 零 shot 基础模型可以支持非平稳云环境中的决策对齐资源供应，同时减少每租户重复训练、验证和部署的运营负担。

Abstract: Cloud virtual machines are often overprovisioned, creating avoidable cost and operational inefficiency. We present CLOUDADV, an interactive engineer-facing advisory system for cloud instance sizing under workload drift. The system combines zero-shot time-series forecasting with bounded recommendation generation across day-, week-, and month-scale planning horizons. For each query, CLOUDADV constructs a structured decision context from historical utilization, forecast summaries, current VM metadata, candidate instance options, pricing, and explicit sizing heuristics. A higher-capacity LLM is used offline to generate reference recommendations, while a smaller production model is evaluated on the same prompts to assess deployment-time alignment under latency and cost constraints. Evaluation prioritizes downstream recommendation quality using simulated Azure cost savings and ex-post exceedance, with rolling-origin forecast accuracy reported as a secondary diagnostic against classical and supervised baselines. In a case study of seven production VMs, the reference recommendations reduce simulated monthly cost from about \$1,503 to \$708, yielding \$795/month in savings (52.9%) under conservative heuristic constraints, while the highest observed exceedance rate among downgraded cases is 1.5%. Although Chronos-2 does not minimize every forecasting metric, it often induces recommendation patterns similar to those of a supervised per-VM baseline. These results suggest that zero-shot foundation models can support decision-aligned provisioning in non-stationary cloud environments while reducing the operational burden of repeated per-tenant retraining, revalidation, and redeployment.

</details>


### [255] [One Reflection Is Not Enough: Self-Correcting Autonomous Research via Multi-Hypothesis Failure Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31478)
*Jie Ma,Binfei Chu,Jie Gao,Jinlu Zhang,Yiwei Ma,Yi Tan,Jiayi Ji,Xiaoshuai Sun,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE通过多假设失败归因（MHFA）机制，将自主研究代理的故障恢复从单一自由形式反思转变为结构化因果诊断，在12主题5领域基准上将指标产出从42%提升至92%，人工评分从5.00提升至6.75/10，并在盲评中超越AI-Scientist-v2（52.0 vs 48.2），同时通过约束报告机制杜绝幻觉数字，显著提升了科学工件的可靠性和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究代理在实验失败时恢复能力脆弱，通常依赖单一的自由形式反思将丰富的轨迹信息压缩为简短的文字批评，导致要么陷入局部试错，要么进行硬性转向丢弃有用上下文，缺乏结构化的故障恢复机制。

Method: 提出SAGE框架，核心是多假设失败归因（MHFA）：将恢复视为结构化因果诊断，通过分析动态轨迹特征系统生成多个基于证据的失败解释，独立评估其严重性，并将验证后的根因确定性路由到正确的干预层级（假设、实验设计或实现）。同时采用接地报告机制，将结果约束为实际测量值，杜绝幻觉数字。

Result: 在12主题5领域基准上：指标产出从42%提升至92%；工件质量从5.00提升至6.75/10；盲评超越AI-Scientist-v2（52.0 vs 48.2）；增益集中在代码开发和执行环节。

Conclusion: 结构化恢复与显式接地约束相结合的方式显著优于单一块反思范式，SAGE为未来自主研究建立了更可靠的信任基础。尽管完全自主的科学写作和生成会议级论文仍是整个领域的公开难题，SAGE在生成更可靠、更高质量的科学工件方面取得了实质性进展。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents can now draft hypotheses, write code, run experiments, and produce papers, but they remain brittle when experiments fail. Under the prevailing paradigm, failure recovery is usually delegated to a single free-form reflection: a rich trajectory of metrics, logs, and design choices is compressed into one verbal critique, which often leads either to localized trial-and-error or to hard pivots that discard useful context. We propose SAGE, a Self-correcting, Autonomous, Grounded Experimenter, to tackle this failure-recovery bottleneck. Its core mechanism, Multi-Hypothesis Failure Attribution (MHFA), treats recovery as a structured causal diagnosis. By analyzing dynamic trajectory features, MHFA systematically generates multiple evidence-grounded explanations for a failure, independently evaluates their severity, and deterministically routes the verified root cause to the correct intervention level (hypothesis, experimental design, or implementation). To guarantee scientific honesty, SAGE further employs a grounded reporting mechanism that explicitly constrains drafted results to actual measured values, redacting hallucinated numbers. On a 12-topic, 5-domain benchmark, SAGE increases metrics-bearing outputs from 42% to 92% over a reflection baseline, improves artifact quality from 5.00 to 6.75/10, and blindly outscores AI-Scientist-v2 (52.0 vs. 48.2), with gains concentrated in code development and execution. While fully autonomous scientific writing and generating conference-ready papers remain notoriously difficult open problems for the entire field, SAGE successfully produces significantly more reliable and higher-quality scientific artifacts. Ultimately, by coupling structured recovery with explicit grounding constraints, SAGE significantly outperforms monolithic reflection paradigms, establishing a highly trustworthy foundation for future autonomous research.

</details>


### [256] [Surprise as a Signal for Plasticity and Metacognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31495)
*Louis Mouchon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一思想：由冻结编码器潜在空间中的小型预测器计算的预测误差信号，既可以作为可塑性的门控信号，也可以作为元认知的基础。作者在两个系统中验证了这一想法：1）非参数化 episodic memory 系统，仅在惊讶度高时写入新概念，并通过周期性离线回放巩固记忆，在 ImageNet 1000类持续学习任务中显著提升旧类保留率；2）视觉-语言模型系统，利用相同惊讶信号调节模型行为（自信回答、模糊回答或拒绝回答），外部检测器AUROC达0.966，睡眠阶段后能回忆99.2%的教授事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索预测误差信号（由小型预测器在冻结编码器潜在空间上计算）能否同时作为可塑性门控信号和元认知的底层机制，从而在持续学习和交互式学习场景中实现高效记忆管理与行为调控。

Method: 提出了两种系统：1）持续学习系统：使用冻结的DINOv2或I-JEPA骨干网络，小型预测器计算潜在空间中的预测误差作为惊讶度，episodic memory仅在惊讶度高时写入新概念，周期性离线回放将近期记忆整合到慢速线性读出中；2）视觉-语言模型系统：在共享文本-图像空间中计算惊讶信号，调节模型对已知、部分熟悉和全新概念的响应行为（自信/模糊/拒绝），并通过睡眠阶段清空快速存储并将知识整合到慢速存储中。

Result: 持续学习系统：在1000类ImageNet持续流中，回放阶段对DINOv2恢复17.7点、对I-JEPA恢复51.3点的旧类保留率；消融实验显示仅回放近期窗口比不回放更差。少量学习任务：5-way 1-shot mini-ImageNet达91.6%。VLM系统：外部检测器区分已知与新颖概念的AUROC为0.966（模型自身语言化置信度仅0.618）；睡眠阶段后系统能回忆99.2%的教授事实，而基础模型为0%。

Conclusion: 两个系统均作为概念验证报告，明确指出了局限性。第二个系统与近期episodic memory和个性化VLM工作进行了对比定位，表明预测误差信号在为视觉-语言模型提供元认知能力和持续学习能力方面具有潜力。

Abstract: We study a single idea across two settings: that a prediction-error signal, computed by a small predictor over the latent space of a frozen encoder, can serve both as a gate on plasticity and as a substrate for metacognition. In the first system, a non-parametric episodic memory writes a new concept only when this surprise is high, and a periodic offline replay phase consolidates recent traces into a slow linear readout. On a continual stream of 1000 ImageNet classes with a frozen DINOv2 or I-JEPA backbone, the consolidation phase recovers 17.7 points of retention on the oldest classes for DINOv2 and 51.3 points for I-JEPA (single-seed runs), and an ablation shows that replaying only a recent window is worse than no replay at all. In few-shot evaluation the same memory reaches 91.6% on 5-way 1-shot mini-ImageNet, above a task-specific baseline, while a harder 500-way regime exposes the true difficulty. In the second system, the same surprise signal, computed in a shared text-image space, modulates the behaviour of a vision-language model: it answers assertively when a concept is known, hedges when it is partially familiar, and refuses to identify the object and asks for an explanation when it is novel, learning the concept from a single user utterance. The external detector separates known from novel concepts at an AUROC of 0.966 (95% CI +/-0.024), far above the model's own verbalised confidence (0.618), while its token-level confidence sits below chance under greedy decoding; after a sleep phase that empties the fast store, the system recalls 99.2% of fifty taught facts from the consolidated store while a base model recovers none. We report both systems as proof-of-concept, with explicit limitations, and position the second against recent episodic-memory and personalised-VLM work.

</details>


### [257] [Design and Implementation of Agentic Orchestrations and Orchestration of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31518)
*Stefanie Rinderle-Ma,Juergen Mangler,Johannes Loebbecke,Dominik Voigt,Nataliia Klievtsova,Matthias Ehrendorfer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了代理业务流程管理的分类框架，从任务特异性、可追溯性、自治性、正确性保证等维度对代理编排方案进行分类，并提供了定性和定量评估标准，通过预测性光感场景的多种代理实现进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的AI代理在业务流程管理中的应用日益增多，如何平衡代理的自主性与流程技术带来的鲁棒性、可追溯性和可跟踪性成为一个关键问题。目前缺乏系统性的框架来分类和评估不同的代理编排方案。

Method: 提出了一个多维度的分类框架，涵盖任务特异性、可追溯性与可跟踪性、自主性与反应性、正确性保证等属性；同时提供了定性决策标准和定量评估指标，并通过预测性光感场景的多种代理实现进行实例化验证。

Result: 建立了代理编排方案的系统分类框架，定义了可量化的评估指标，并通过具体场景验证了框架的可用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为代理编排的设计与实现提供了系统的属性、标准和度量方法，有助于在代理自主性与流程可控性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Agentic Business Process Management has gained momentum recently. The prospect is that the autonomy of AI agents, i.e., predominantly LLM-based agents, can be balanced with a certain level of robustness, tractability, and traceability through a combination with process technology. In this paper, we provide a classification framework for agentic orchestration options along properties such as task specificity, traceability and tractability, autonomy and reactivity, and correctness assurance and present qualitative decision criteria for realizations of different scenarios. We also provide metrics for the quantitative assessment of realization properties and show them through different agentic implementations of a predictive light sensing scenario. Altogether, this work aims at providing properties, criteria, and metrics for the design and implementation of agentic orchestrations and orchestration of agents.

</details>


### [258] [A time-series classification framework for individual-level absenteeism prediction under severe class imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31532)
*Kwong Ho Li,Matthew Roughan,Wathsala Karunarathne*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有回归/分类方法无法实现前瞻性个体缺勤预测的问题，本文提出基于时间序列分类（TSC）的框架，利用历史考勤序列预测未来缺勤，在模拟数据集上评估了BFL和G-Mean损失函数及三种深度学习架构，发现LSTM-FCN在精度和特异性上表现最佳，平衡准确率约达80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 员工缺勤给医疗、急救、建筑等高需求工作环境带来高昂运营成本，现有回归和分类方法存在结构性缺陷：它们将t时刻的特征映射到t时刻的标签，复现已发生的结果而非预测未来事件，且丢弃了个体考勤历史中固有的序列行为结构。

Method: 提出时间序列分类（TSC）框架，将历史考勤序列与未来缺勤标签分离以实现前瞻性预测。构建了可复现的模拟数据集（校准至UCI数据集）。分析了二元焦点损失（BFL）和几何均值（G-Mean）损失在严重类别不平衡下的表现；评估了LSTM、CNN及混合LSTM-FCN三种深度学习架构。

Result: BFL的性能主要由平衡权重α控制，达到特异性0.813和平衡准确率0.888，与G-Mean相当；G-Mean无需参数校准。LSTM-FCN在精度和特异性上表现优异；批次大小≥64、窗口大小40-80天时性能稳定，在留出测试集上平衡准确率约达80%。

Conclusion: 所提出的TSC框架能够有效实现前瞻性个体缺勤预测；G-Mean因自动适应不平衡而优于需调参的BFL；LSTM-FCN是推荐的架构选择。

Abstract: Staff absenteeism imposes substantial operational costs in high-demand work environments such as healthcare, emergency services, meat processing, construction, and courier and delivery services, where proactive workforce planning depends on reliable individual-level absence prediction. Existing regression and classification approaches share a structural limitation; they map features observed at time t to labels at the same time t, reproducing already-realised outcomes rather than predicting future events, and discard the sequential behavioural structure inherent in individual attendance histories. We propose a Time Series Classification (TSC) framework that separates historical attendance sequences from future absence labels, enabling genuinely proactive prediction. Due to the lack of public longitudinal attendance data, we construct a reproducible simulated dataset calibrated to the UCI dataset. We analyse Binary Focal Loss (BFL) and Geometric Mean (G-Mean) loss under severe class imbalance using only the imbalance ratio $ρ$. For BFL, the initial gradient ratio is $ρα/(1-α)$, implying the balanced weight $α= 1/(1+ρ) \approx 0.023$. Experiments show that performance is governed mainly by $α$, with BFL achieving specificity 0.813 and balanced accuracy 0.888, comparable to G-Mean. Unlike BFL, G-Mean adapts automatically without parameter calibration. Among three deep learning architectures evaluated, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), Convolutional Neural Network (CNN), and the hybrid LSTM-Fully Convolutional Network (LSTM-FCN), the LSTM-FCN delivers strong precision and specificity. Stable performance is obtained with batch sizes >= 64 and window sizes between 40-80 days, yielding balanced accuracy of approximately 80% on held-out test data.

</details>


### [259] [Modality-Driven Search with Holistic Trace Judging for ARC-AGI-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31543)
*Johan Land*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多模态推理搜索与整体性评判的ARC-AGI-2求解器，在验证集上以72.9%准确率超越GPT-5.2 Pro等前沿模型，每任务成本38.99美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在抽象推理任务中能生成流畅但自信错误的推理踪迹，因此候选方案的筛选比生成更具挑战性，现有自一致性或多数投票方法在多数答案错误时失效。

Method: 两大原则：(i) 将推理模态视为搜索算子，在文本、图像、代码三个通道独立生成多样化候选方案；(ii) 上下文保留的整体性评判，由评判模型在单个长上下文提示中联合比较所有候选推理踪迹。

Result: ARC Prize半私有验证集72.9%（38.99美元/任务），超越GPT-5.2 Pro（54.2%）和Gemini 3 Pro（54.0%）18.7个百分点；公开评估集76.1%（19.69美元/任务）。

Conclusion: 多模态独立生成+整体性评判能可靠恢复少数正确假设，且发现规定性提示模板和迭代式精炼会系统性降低假设多样性并损害性能，开源全部代码。

Abstract: Large language models can produce fluent, internally coherent reasoning traces for abstract reasoning tasks while still being confidently wrong - making selection among candidates, not just generation, the central challenge. I present a solver for ARC-AGI-2, a few-shot visual reasoning benchmark, built around two principles: (i) treating reasoning modalities as search operators, generating diverse candidates independently across text, image, and code channels, and (ii) context-preserving holistic judging, in which a judge model jointly compares all candidate reasoning traces within a single long-context prompt. Unlike self-consistency or majority voting, this approach reliably recovers correct minority hypotheses on tasks where the modal answer is wrong.
  On the ARC Prize semi-private evaluation set, the solver achieves 72.9 percent at USD 38.99 per task - the highest score on the verified leaderboard at the time of writing, exceeding the best standalone frontier models, GPT-5.2 Pro at 54.2 percent and Gemini 3 Pro at 54.0 percent, by +18.7 percentage points. On the public evaluation set, it achieves 76.1 percent at USD 19.69 per task. I release the full source code and document extensive negative results, including the finding that prescriptive prompting templates and iterative refinement systematically reduce hypothesis diversity and degrade performance.

</details>


### [260] [ACE: Pluggable Adaptive Context Elasticizer across Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31564)
*Ning Liao,Zihao Long,Xiaoxing Wang,Xue Yang,Yaoming Wang,Ziyuan Zhuang,Xunliang Cai,Rongxiang Weng,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ACE是一种即插即用的上下文弹性编排模块，通过无损消息维护层和上下文编排层，在LLM智能体决策时动态为历史步骤分配原始/抽象/丢弃三种弹性类型，实现可逆的上下文管理，在四种智能体框架上均优于截断和摘要基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有截断和摘要等上下文管理技术存在固有的僵化和不可逆问题——一旦信息被丢弃或压缩，即使后续决策步骤中变得关键相关也无法恢复，无法应对智能体任务轨迹不断增长带来的挑战。

Method: ACE包含两个核心层：(1)无损消息维护层——存储每个历史步骤的原始消息和压缩抽象；(2)上下文编排层——基于当前任务状态为每个步骤动态分配弹性类型（原始/抽象/丢弃），实现可逆的紧凑上下文构建。

Result: 在ReAct、DeepAgent、WebThinker、MiroFlow四种不同智能体框架上，ACE一致优于截断和摘要基线，并带来稳定的性能提升。

Conclusion: ACE作为无需训练或架构修改的即插即用模块，通过可逆的上下文弹性编排有效解决了长轨迹场景下LLM智能体的上下文管理难题。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of agentic tasks has led to rapidly growing trajectory lengths, which poses significant challenges for large language model (LLM) based agents with fixed context windows. Existing context management techniques, such as truncation and summarization, suffer from inherent inflexibility and irreversibility: once information is discarded or compressed, it cannot be recovered even when it becomes critically relevant in later decision steps. To address these limitations, we propose the Adaptive Context Elasticizer (ACE), a plug-and-play module that elastically orchestrates historical step information into the agent's context at each decision step. ACE maintains a lossless message maintenance layer that stores both raw messages and compressed abstractions for each historical step, while a context orchestration layer adaptively assigns each step an elastic type as raw, abstract, or drop, at every decision step based on the current task state. This reversible design ensures that the main LLM always receives a compact yet information-rich context. We adapt ACE to four diverse agent frameworks, including ReAct, DeepAgent, WebThinker, and MiroFlow, without training or architectural modifications. Experiments show that ACE consistently outperforms truncation and summarization baselines, and brings consistent performance gains across all four agent frameworks.

</details>


### [261] [Which Tokens Matter? Adaptive Token Selection for RLVR with the Relative Surprisal Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31575)
*Outongyi Lv,Yanzhao Zheng,Yuanwei Zhang,Zhenghao Huang,Xingjun Wang,Baohua Dong,Hangcheng Zhu,Yingda Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出相对惊奇指数（RSI），一种信息论度量，通过耦合token熵与选中token概率来统一解释RLVR中高熵与低概率两种看似矛盾的成功范式，并基于RSI提出RSI-S过滤方法，在AIME和AMC基准上相较GRPO提升2-3%的avg@32准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）虽在LLM推理中取得成功，但现有研究提供了两种看似矛盾的见解：一种主张优先训练高熵token位置，另一种警告不应让低概率token主导梯度更新。作者认为孤立评估采样token概率或熵不足以捕捉策略优化动态，因此需要一种统一的理论框架来化解这一张力。

Method: 1. 提出相对惊奇指数（RSI），一种信息论度量，自然耦合token熵与选中token概率；2. 证明在温和条件下RSI与logit梯度范数和预测熵在选定logit扰动下的一阶变分之局部比值相关；3. 基于RSI提出RSI-S（RSI Selection），一种熵自适应token过滤方法，仅保留处于稳定RSI区间内的token，同时过滤冗余的低惊奇token和不稳定的高惊奇尾部token。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B、3B和7B三个模型规模上，在AIME和AMC基准测试中，RSI-S相较于GRPO在avg@32准确率上提升了2-3个百分点。

Conclusion: RSI为RLVR改进提供了一个有前景的新视角，成功调和了前人看似矛盾的训练范式，验证了熵自适应token筛选在强化学习微调中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a powerful tool for propelling Large Language Models (LLMs) beyond imitation-based training towards more robust reasoning capabilities. Among existing approaches, RL with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a pivotal paradigm for advancing LLM reasoning. Despite its empirical success, recent studies have offered different insights. One line of inquiry advocates prioritizing high-entropy token positions during training, while another perspective cautions against allowing low-probability tokens to dominate gradient updates. Notably, although high-entropy tokens are usually correlated with low probability, both paradigms empirically yield substantial performance gains. In this work, we argue that evaluating sampled-token probability or entropy in isolation is insufficient to capture the policy optimization dynamics. To resolve this tension, we introduce the Relative Surprisal Index (RSI), a principled, information-theoretic metric that naturally couples the token's entropy with the probability of the selected token. We show that, under mild conditions, RSI is related to the local ratio between the first-order variations of the logit-gradient norm and predictive entropy under a selected-logit perturbation. Building on RSI, we propose RSI Selection (RSI-S), an entropy-adaptive token filtering method that retains tokens within a stable RSI interval. RSI-S successfully reconciles previous contradictory paradigms and filters out both redundant low-surprisal tokens and unstable high-surprisal tail tokens. Empirical evaluations show that RSI-S achieves higher avg@32 accuracy across different model scales (Qwen2.5-1.5B, 3B, and 7B) on AIME and AMC benchmarks: RSI-S improves avg@32 accuracy by 2--3 percentage points over GRPO. Overall, RSI offers a promising perspective for RLVR improvement.

</details>


### [262] [Scientific Explanations in Health Sciences: Causality, Trust, and Epistemic Adequacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31616)
*Martina Mattioli,Marcello Pelillo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从科学哲学视角批判性审视可解释人工智能（XAI）在医学领域的基础假设，认为当前XAI研究忽视了哲学界对"解释"的长期探讨。通过分析健康科学中的解释标准，提出因果关系、医学信任的认识论与关系维度、以及不同利益相关者的解释充分性标准，是构建医学XAI系统的三大核心分析轴，旨在为临床决策提供认识论上稳健且实践上充分的解释框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学XAI研究对"何为恰当的解释"这一基本问题缺乏共识，且忽视了科学哲学与医学哲学领域关于"解释"的成熟理论成果，导致其基础假设未经充分审视。本文旨在填补这一跨学科空白。

Method: 采用批判性综述方法，在科学哲学与XAI的交叉点上展开分析：审视健康科学中关于"解释"的主流观点，评估其对医学XAI的适用性，并基于哲学文献识别出三大核心分析轴（因果性、信任的认识论与关系维度、解释充分性的实用标准）。

Result: 指出医学XAI系统设计应遵循的原则：解释不仅需要认识论上的稳健性，还需符合临床决策的认识论与实践要求；明确三大核心分析轴为构建哲学上有根据的医学可解释性方法提供了必要条件。

Conclusion: 通过整合哲学分析与医学AI发展现状，本文为医学XAI系统提出了设计原则，推动该领域关注此前被忽视的概念基础，使解释既具有认识论可靠性，又满足多元化利益相关者的实用需求。

Abstract: Medical Artificial Intelligence (AI) is widely expected to transform clinical practice, yet the decision-making processes of many Machine Learning (ML) models remain opaque. Explainability has been advanced as a partial remedy to clarify why AI generates predictions, particularly in high-stakes contexts. Despite ongoing efforts, debates on what constitutes an adequate medical explanation remain unsettled. Yet, explanation has long been a central topic of inquiry in the philosophy of science and medicine. The insights developed in these fields, however, have been largely overlooked in contemporary explainable AI (XAI) research, leaving its foundational assumptions insufficiently examined. To address this gap, this paper develops a critical review at the intersection of philosophy of science and XAI. It examines prevailing accounts of what counts as an explanation in the health sciences and assesses their adequacy for informing XAI in medicine, arguing that they provide necessary conditions for a philosophically grounded approach to explainability in this domain. Building on this foundational philosophical literature, the discussion identifies three central axes of analysis: the role of causality in medical reasoning, the epistemic and relational dimensions of medical trust, and the criteria of explanatory adequacy as shaped by the pragmatic needs of diverse stakeholders. By integrating philosophical analysis with current developments in medical AI, the paper outlines principles for designing XAI systems that offer explanations that are not only epistemically robust but also aligned with the epistemic and practical requirements of clinical decision-making, shaping ongoing debates in medical XAI toward underexplored conceptual foundations.

</details>


### [263] [Think in English, Answer in Korean: Efficient Adaptation of Multilingual Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31648)
*Utsav Garg,Sungjin Hong,Jason Jung,Justin Lee,Shaan Desai,Joon Hee Kim,Anirudh Shrinivason,Edmond Wen,Susie Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LuckyStar 111B是一个111B参数混合推理模型，由Cohere和LG CNS联合开发，在Command A模型基础上通过前文条件控制、多语言SFT、可验证奖励强化学习、语言一致性奖励和4-bit量化等技术适配，在内存受限的单GPU部署环境下提升了数学推理、函数调用和NL2SQL等工具使用能力，同时保持韩英指令遵循质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内存和服务部署受限的实际企业场景下，如何高效地将已有的多语言后训练模型适配为兼具非推理和工具导向推理能力的智能体模型，同时支持韩英双语的企业应用需求。

Method: 1) 基于Cohere的Command A后训练模型进行继续训练，而非从头预训练；2) 使用前文条件控制（preamble conditioning）在简洁非推理行为与较长工具导向推理行为之间切换；3) 四种缩放策略：多语言监督微调（SFT）、基于可验证奖励的多步工具使用强化学习、面向韩语用户响应的语言一致性奖励、以及单GPU服务的4-bit量化。

Result: 改进的模型在数学推理、函数调用和智能体自然语言到SQL（NL2SQL）任务上性能提升，同时保留了通用的韩语和英语指令遵循质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为在内存受限的部署环境下，将后训练的多语言模型适配到可验证的智能体工作流提供了实用的技术路线和失败模式分析。

Abstract: We present LuckyStar 111B, a 111B-parameter hybrid reasoning model developed through a collaboration between Cohere and LG CNS for Korean-English enterprise agents under practical memory and serving constraints. The model trains from Cohere's fully post-trained Command A model rather than a new pretraining run, and uses preamble conditioning to switch between concise non-reasoning behavior and longer tool-oriented reasoning. We study four choices for scaling tool-using agents efficiently: multilingual supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for multi-step tool-use tasks, language-consistency rewards for Korean user-facing responses, and 4-bit quantization for single-GPU serving. The adapted model improves mathematical reasoning, function calling, and agentic natural-language-to-SQL (NL2SQL) performance while preserving general Korean and English instruction-following quality. These results provide a practical recipe and failure-mode analysis for adapting post-trained multilingual models to verifiable agentic workflows under memory-constrained deployment.

</details>


### [264] [FARS: A Fully Automated Research System Deployed at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31651)
*Qiong Tang,Xiangkun Hu,Xiangyang Liu,Yiran Chen,Yunfan Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FARS是一个全自动AI研究系统，能在67个AI/ML子领域自主生成166篇完整论文，经282份结构化评审显示可产出值得评审的研究成果，但也暴露出实验范围狭窄、方法局限和完整性等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化研究系统大多依赖精选案例、人工设定主题或少数预定义任务，缺乏能够跨研究主题大规模运行的全自动系统，因此需要构建一个能在多个AI/ML研究方向上自主完成从构思到写作全流程的系统。

Method: FARS采用分阶段智能体协同架构，包含构思、规划、实验和写作等阶段专属智能体，通过共享工作空间（记录提案、代码、日志、结果和手稿）进行协调。首次公开部署在67个细粒度AI/ML主题上生成了166篇完整研究论文，并保留了中间产物作为可审计语料库。

Result: 从282份来自志愿评审者的结构化评审（覆盖140篇论文）来看，FARS能够在大规模公开部署中产出值得评审甚至偶尔高质量的AI/ML研究产物；同时暴露出实验范围狭窄、方法论局限和完整性问题等重复性失败模式。

Conclusion: FARS展示了全自动AI研究系统在大规模多主题场景下的可行性和潜力，但也揭示了当前自动化研究在实验设计质量、方法严谨性和结果完整性方面仍存在显著瓶颈，为后续改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Recent automated research systems show that language-model agents can generate hypotheses, run experiments, and write complete manuscripts, but most evidence still comes from selected examples, human-framed topics, or a few pre-defined research tasks. We present FARS (Fully Automated Research System), a fully automated AI-for-AI research system designed to operate across research topics at scale. FARS autonomously generates and advances projects through ideation, planning, experimentation, and writing, using stage-specific agents coordinated through a shared workspace that records proposals, code, logs, results, and manuscripts. In its first public deployment, FARS produced 166 complete research papers spanning 67 fine-grained AI/ML topics while preserving intermediate artifacts as an auditable corpus rather than a curated set of successes. We evaluate this corpus with 282 structured reviews from volunteer reviewers covering 140 papers, including overall ratings, sub-scores, integrity checks, and LLM-use disclosure. The reviews indicate that FARS can produce review-worthy and occasionally strong AI/ML research artifacts in a large-scale public deployment, while also exposing recurring failure modes in narrow experimental scope, methodological limitations, and integrity issues.

</details>


### [265] [Arena-T2I Hard: Benchmarking and Improving Faithfulness with Dependency-Aware Checklist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31711)
*Yuanhao Ban,Tong Xie,Sohyun An,Yunqi Hong,Evan Frick,I-Hung Hsu,Wei-Lin Chiang,Ion Stoica,Cho-Jui Hsieh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Arena-T2I Hard基准测试，包含310个高难度提示词，每个提示词分解为约30个细粒度约束（六类），用于评估T2I模型的忠实度。研究发现现有排名无法预测忠实度，并提出了依赖感知的检查表奖励（DAG结构）结合组解耦标准化（GDPO）的强化学习方法，实现了更好的忠实度-美学权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有忠实度基准依赖简单原子指令，顶级模型已接近满分；而实际用户需求是多方面且复杂的（空间关系、风格约束、文本渲染等），单一二值VLM评分无法捕捉模型在哪些具体约束上失败。

Method: 1) 构建Arena-T2I Hard基准：从真实竞技场日志中筛选310个高难度提示词，每个分解为约30个是/否约束（六类）。2) 提出依赖感知检查表奖励：将提示词分解为DAG（有向无环图）的是/否问题，父节点失败则子节点归零。3) 提出组解耦标准化（GDPO）：将每个奖励在其rollout组内标准化，防止单一奖励崩溃。

Result: 最强闭源系统得分为0.855，11个系统间存在33个百分点的性能差距。高公共竞技场排名无法预测忠实度（BT偏好评分优先考虑美学而非细粒度提示遵循）。GDPO方法在SD3.5-Medium和FLUX.1-dev上取得了比所有单奖励、朴素加权和或4奖励BT集成基线更优的忠实度-美学权衡。

Conclusion: T2I模型的忠实度评估需要细粒度、多约束的基准；提出的DAG检查表奖励和GDPO方法可以有效平衡忠实度与美学性能，且不需要多奖励集成。

Abstract: Faithfulness -- how precisely a generated image aligns with its prompt -- is increasingly central to the real-world utility of text-to-image (T2I) models. Existing faithfulness benchmarks, however, rely on simple atomic instructions, on which top-tier systems already achieve near-perfect scores. As T2I models enter creative workflows, users issue multi-faceted requests combining intricate spatial relationships, stylistic constraints, and complex text rendering. In this setting, a single binary VLM-judge score no longer captures which specific constraints the model fails to satisfy. We introduce Arena-T2I Hard, a 310-prompt stress benchmark drawn from real arena T2I logs, with approximately 30 decomposed yes/no constraints per prompt spanning six categories, including text rendering. The strongest closed-source system we evaluate reaches 0.855 with a 33~pp performance gap across 11 systems, demonstrating substantial discriminative power. Moreover, high public-arena rankings fail to predict faithfulness, confirming that holistic Bradley-Terry (BT) preference scores prioritize aesthetics over fine-grained prompt adherence. We propose a dependency-aware checklist reward that decomposes each prompt into a DAG of yes/no questions and zeroes descendants of failed parents, turning faithfulness into a per-constraint training signal. Combined with a BT aesthetic reward via group-decoupled normalization (GDPO), which standardizes each reward within its rollout group so neither collapses, the recipe attains a strictly better faithfulness-aesthetics trade-off on SD3.5-Medium and FLUX.1-dev under MMRB2 pairwise comparisons than every single-reward, naive weighted-sum, or 4-reward BT-ensemble baseline.

</details>


### [266] [A Self-Evolving Agentic System for Automated Generation and Execution of Biological Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31763)
*Yankai Jiang,Weiting Tang,Haoran Sun,Zhenyu Tang,Yuejie Hou,Yingnan Han,Rubo Wang,Yueyuxiao Yang,Cheng Liang,Lilong Wang,Wenjie Lou,Xiaosong Wang,Lei Bai,Meng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProtoPilot是一个自进化多智能体系统，结合专家基准评估框架，能将生物协议自动转换为可执行的实验代码，在294个合成/分子生物学任务中实现了90.2%的专家偏好率和88.24%的Opentrons通过率，并通过湿实验验证了反馈修正的自动化实验路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主湿实验自动化需要协议文本、定量流程、设备约束与实验反馈之间的全程对齐，现有方法仅生成看似合理的协议文本而缺乏可执行性保障，因此需要建立从协议设计到代码执行再到物理实验的完整自动化转换与验证框架。

Method: ProtoPilot采用多智能体编排架构，包含分层可验证性机制、运行时更新的技能库，能够生成协议、扩展SOP、合成符合SDK的代码并根据湿实验反馈修正工作流。配套构建了涵盖294个任务的专家基准评估框架，包含湿实验专家评分、设备级有效性门控和真实实验测试。

Result: Top@3专家偏好率90.2%，协议到代码门控通过率89.5%，Opentrons通过率88.24%（对比OpenTrons-AI的32.35%）。湿实验验证产出可解释读数、Sanger测序确认产物及反馈修正的PCA组装DNA靶标。

Conclusion: 评估框架能够捕获自主湿实验自动化所需的执行相关需求，ProtoPilot通过将协议和代码生成转化为可验证执行与反馈引导修正，建立了通往自主实验的可靠路径。

Abstract: Autonomous wet-lab experimentation requires more than plausible protocol text: biological intent, quantitative procedures, device constraints and experimental feedback must remain aligned from protocol and SOP design to code and physical execution. We developed ProtoPilot, a self-evolving multi-agent system, together with an expert-grounded benchmark and evaluation framework for testing this conversion as an experimental automation problem. The framework spans 294 synthetic-biology and molecular-biology tasks derived from 98 gold-standard protocols, wet-lab expert rubrics, device-level validity gates and real experimental tests. ProtoPilot incorporates layer-wise verifiability, multi-agent orchestration and a runtime-updated skill library to generate protocols, expand SOPs, synthesize SDK-compliant code and revise workflows from wet-lab feedback. It achieved a Top@3 expert-preference rate of 90.2%, an overall protocol-to-code gate pass rate of 89.5% and an Opentrons pass rate of 88.24%, compared with 32.35% for OpenTrons-AI. Wet-lab validation produced interpretable readouts, Sanger-confirmed products and feedback-corrected PCA-assembled DNA targets, establishing a verifiable route to autonomous experimentation. Together, these results show that the evaluation framework captures execution-relevant requirements for autonomous wet-lab automation, and that ProtoPilot can meet them by converting protocol and code generation into validated execution and feedback-guided revision.

</details>


### [267] [Evo-PI: Aligning Medical Reasoning via Evolving Principle-Guided Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31800)
*Xianda Zheng,Huan Gao,Meng-Fen Chiang,Michael Witbrock,Kaiqi Zhao,Shangyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Evo-PI，一种将推理原则作为可进化语言监督信号的框架，通过原则与模型行为的共同进化实现动态对齐，在医疗视觉问答等任务中显著提升推理准确率（最高提升24.6%），为多模态大模型的推理训练提供了可扩展的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs依赖静态监督（固定提示、规则或奖励模型），这种非自适应的监督信号虽然能约束输出格式，但无法塑造底层推理过程，导致泛化能力脆弱、在复杂决策任务中性能饱和。

Method: 提出Evo-PI框架，将推理原则作为显式的、基于语言的监督信号，支持原则的生成、评估和迭代进化。通过原则指导模型推理、模型行为反向优化原则的共进化循环，实现监督信号对模型推理缺陷的动态自适应对齐。

Result: 在医疗视觉问答的8个基准测试和多个模型骨干上，Evo-PI一致性地提升了推理准确率，最高提升达24.6%。

Conclusion: 进化的原则引导监督为训练专家级对齐推理提供了一种可扩展且通用的范式，有望突破静态监督的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Despite recent progress, the reasoning capabilities of large multimodal language models (MLLMs) remain fundamentally constrained by static supervision, where fixed prompts, rules, or reward models provide non-adaptive guidance throughout training. Such static signals are often sufficient to enforce output formats, but fail to shape the underlying reasoning process, leading to brittle generalization and performance saturation in complex decision-making tasks. We propose Evo-PI, a principle-centric learning framework that treats reasoning principles as explicit, language-based supervision signals that can be generated, evaluated, and iteratively evolved. Instead of relying on fixed rewards, Evo-PI enables a co-evolutionary loop in which principles guide model reasoning, while model behaviors in turn refine the principles that supervise them. This dynamic alignment mechanism allows supervision to progressively adapt to the model's reasoning deficiencies. We instantiate Evo-PI in medical visual question answering as a high-stakes testbed requiring structured visual-textual reasoning. Across eight benchmarks and multiple model backbones, Evo-PI consistently improves reasoning accuracy, achieving gains of up to 24.6%. Our results suggest that evolving principle-guided supervision offers a scalable and general paradigm for training expert-aligned reasoning in MLLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/zhengxianda/Evo_PI.

</details>


### [268] [RAISE: LLM-based Automated Heuristic Design with Robust Adversary Instance Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31801)
*Fei Liu,Alessio Figalli,Patrick Owen,Nicola Serra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有基于LLM的自动启发式设计方法在训练实例上表现优异，但遇到分布偏移时会严重退化（性能下降可达19倍）。本文提出RAISE框架，通过在外层LLM进化搜索中嵌入内层无LLM的对抗实例搜索（在训练集ε邻域内寻找最困难实例），实现了鲁棒的启发式设计。在在线装箱、在线作业车间调度和在线车辆路径问题上的实验表明，RAISE能在多种分布偏移下保持稳定性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM自动启发式设计方法仅在固定训练实例集上优化，忽视了真实世界中的分布偏移问题，导致部署时性能急剧下降（最多19倍）。需要一种能在训练分布邻域内保持鲁棒性的启发式生成方法。

Method: 提出RAISE框架，采用双层优化结构：外层利用LLM算子（如变异、交叉）进化搜索启发式；内层使用无LLM的高效算法，在以训练实例集为中心的ε-ball邻域内，通过基分布参数化与边界投影方法快速寻找最困难的对抗实例。

Result: 在在线装箱、在线作业车间调度、在线车辆路径三个问题及五种分布族上的实验表明，现有LLM-AHD方法在分布偏移下性能下降最多19倍，而RAISE在所有测试分布和问题规模下均能保持稳定的强性能。

Conclusion: 将对抗实例搜索融入LLM进化循环是提升自动启发式设计鲁棒性的有效途径；无LLM的内层搜索策略在保证效率的同时提供了对分布偏移的实质性防护。

Abstract: Automated Heuristic Design (AHD) with Large Language Models (LLMs) has shown remarkable progress in discovering high-quality heuristics. However, existing LLM-based AHD methods optimize heuristics for a fixed training instance set and may fail catastrophically when deployed under real-world distributional shifts. We propose Robust Adversary Instance Search (RAISE), a framework that integrates constrained worst-case instance search within a principled neighborhood of the training distribution into the LLM-based evolutionary search loop. RAISE treats robust AHD as a constrained adversarial instance search problem: the outer loop evolves heuristics via LLM operators, while an LLM-free inner loop efficiently identifies hard instances within an epsilon-ball around the training instance set using a basis distribution parameterization with boundary projection. Comprehensive experiments on Online Bin Packing (OBP), Online Job Shop Scheduling (OJSP), and Online Vehicle Routing (OVRP) across five distribution families demonstrate that existing LLM-based AHD methods degrade by up to 19 times under distribution shift, while RAISE consistently maintains strong performance across all tested distributions and problem scales

</details>


### [269] [Large Databases Need Small, Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31808)
*Parker Glenn,Alfy Samuel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明，在仅16GB VRAM的本地设备上运行的量化开源模型，在LM增强型数据库操作中，能够以更低的延迟和成本（成本降低390倍，延迟降低3.8倍）达到或超越闭源LM API的精度，挑战了闭源API在LM-DB集成中不可或缺的普遍假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于专有API的语言模型系统在大规模数据库场景下成本过高（单组实验超1万美元）的问题，打破闭源LM API对LM-DB集成领域的主导假设。

Method: 采用量化的开源权重模型在16GB VRAM本地运行，通过关键系统优化高效部署，并将其集成到BlendSQL v0.1.0框架中。

Result: 相比专有LM API，整体成本降低390倍，延迟降低3.8倍，同时精度持平或更优。

Conclusion: 量化开源模型在LM-DB系统中可替代昂贵的闭源API，为大规模数据库场景下的LM集成提供了经济高效的可行方案。

Abstract: Language model systems built around proprietary APIs often operate on a token-based cost model. This becomes prohibitively expensive in the context of large databases, where LM-enhanced relational operators can incur costs exceeding $10,000 for a single set of experiments, hindering thorough research and practical deployment. In this paper, we demonstrate that quantized, open-weight models running locally on just 16GB of VRAM can match or exceed the accuracy of closed-source counterparts at lower latency and a fraction of the price, challenging the prevailing assumption that closed-source LM APIs are necessary for effective LM-database integration. We present and analyze the key system optimizations required to efficiently deploy these open-weight models within an LM-DB system. By integrating these local models into the BlendSQL v0.1.0 framework, we demonstrate a 390x reduction in overall costs and 3.8x reduction in latency compared to a proprietary LM API. We make our code available at https://github.com/CapitalOne-Research/play-by-the-type-rules/tree/main/sembench.

</details>


### [270] [Creating Intelligence: A Computational Foundation for AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31819)
*Peter Overmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于集合论和超维计算的新计算心智理论，使用稀疏二进制数据和离散集表示信息，通过拓扑可塑性而非权重调整驱动学习，以子集模式匹配为核心机制实现联想记忆，实现常数时间复杂度，并映射到小脑和新皮质的神经解剖结构，可转化为内存计算硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络依赖连续权重和矩阵乘法，与生物神经系统的离散、稀疏编码方式存在根本差异，需要一种更符合生物学原理的计算理论来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 采用稀疏二进制数据和集合表示信息，通过组合扩展隐藏层的网络拓扑结构自然产生联想记忆，以拓扑可塑性替代标量权重调整作为学习机制，以子集模式匹配和精确最近邻搜索作为统一的信息检索算法。

Result: 该架构统一了自联想和异联想学习，在常数时间复杂度下运行，弥合了感知数据（稀疏分布式表示）与符号（稀疏全息表示）之间的鸿沟，并可直接转化为内存计算硬件。

Conclusion: 子集模式匹配是认知的基本引擎，该框架为构建具有人类级能效的合成智能开辟了新路径。

Abstract: This work introduces a new computational theory of mind grounded in set theory and hyperdimensional computing. Whereas traditional neural networks rely on continuous weights and matrix multiplication, this framework works with sparse binary data. It represents information as discrete sets, directly modeling biological neural population codes. I demonstrate that associative memory emerges naturally from network topologies featuring a combinatorially expanded hidden layer. Learning is driven by topological plasticity rather than scalar weight adjustments. This architecture unifies auto-associative and hetero-associative learning under a single core algorithm: information retrieval via subset pattern matching and exact nearest-neighbor search. Operating with constant-time complexity, these mechanisms bridge perceptual data (sparse distributed representations) and symbols (sparse holographic representations) without continuous bottlenecks. Mapping this framework to neuroanatomy, I propose that both the cerebellum and the neocortex implement variants of this algorithm, making subset pattern matching the fundamental engine of cognition. Because it relies on discrete logic rather than matrix arithmetic, this algorithm translates directly into in-memory hardware. This opens a new route toward synthetic intelligence with human-level energy efficiency.

</details>


### [271] [Adaptive Cluster-First Route-Second Decomposition for Industrial-Scale Vehicle Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31820)
*Oguzhan Karaahmetoglu,Hyong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应的大规模CVRP分解系统，利用大语言模型作为高层决策者，通过迭代式聚类、平衡和细化操作实现问题分解，在50万客户规模的实例上展现出良好的可扩展性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CFRS方法（基于固定划分规则、预定义优化目标或学习策略）在不同空间、需求和操作特征的实例上表现不一致，缺乏自适应性。

Method: 将分解过程建模为迭代决策过程，使用LLM作为高层决策者分析分解状态并选择性应用聚类、平衡和细化算子；联合划分客户和车辆，实现容量感知的聚类。

Result: 在合成和基准CVRP实例（含50万客户）上评估，在基准规模实例上具竞争力，在大规模问题上展现出更好的可扩展性和鲁棒的路径质量。

Conclusion: 自适应、LLM引导的决策支持方法为工业级车辆路径规划和超大规模物流规划提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Large-scale capacitated vehicle routing problems (CVRPs) are commonly addressed using cluster-first route-second (CFRS) approaches that split a routing instance into smaller, computationally tractable subproblems. Existing splitting methods typically rely on fixed partitioning rules, predefined optimization objectives, or learned policies, which may perform inconsistently across instances exhibiting different spatial, demand, and operational characteristics. In this work, we propose an adaptive CFRS system that formulates a decomposition procedure as an iterative decision-making process. Motivated by the recent success of large language models (LLMs) in reasoning and tool selection, the system employs an LLM as a high-level decision maker that analyzes the evolving decomposition state and selectively applies further clustering, balancing, and refinement operators. The proposed algorithm jointly partitions customers and vehicles, enabling capacity-aware clustering while adapting partitioning decisions to the characteristics of each problem. We evaluate the approach on synthetic and benchmark-derived CVRP instances containing up to 500,000 customers. Experimental results demonstrate competitive performance on benchmark-scale instances while exhibiting improved scalability and robust routing quality on substantially larger problems. These results highlight the potential of adaptive, LLM-guided decision support as a practical approach for industrial-scale vehicle routing and large-scale logistics planning.

</details>


### [272] [An Agentic AI Framework to Accelerate Scientific Discovery in Plant Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31831)
*Renan Souza,Daniel Rosendo,Kelsey Carter,John Lagergren,Frédéric Suter,Shelaine L. Curd,Gerald A. Tuskan,Rafael Ferreira da Silva,David Weston*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个端到端的代理型AI框架，将高通量植物表型设施从数据工厂转变为交互式自主发现平台。通过对话式协同科学家代理和计算代理的协作，在Frontier超级计算机上实现秒级的表型分析，将数天到数周的分析流程压缩为交互式循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量植物表型技术生成图像数据的速度远超科学家分析能力，表型特征提取和解释仍依赖人工专家且严格事后进行，分析而非数据采集成为科学发现的瓶颈约束。

Method: 提出了一个端到端的代理型AI框架：1）对话式协同科学家代理将科学家的自然语言问题转化为结构化分析计划；2）无头计算代理在Frontier百亿亿次超级计算机上执行Vision Transformer分割和特征提取；3）两个代理运行在独立的安全和资源域，通过安全令牌认证的流式通道通信，捕获端到端的数据溯源。

Result: 将原本数天到数周的分析过程压缩为交互式循环，代理能在数秒内推理结果、推荐后续分析并响应追问问题。

Conclusion: 该框架考虑了云原生代理框架所忽视的联邦、数据移动和溯源现实需求，通过安全隔离的代理通信设计确保了每次交互的端到端溯源性，将设施从数据工厂转化为交互式自主发现平台。

Abstract: High-throughput plant phenotyping now generates image derived datasets far faster than scientists can analyze them. At Oak Ridge National Laboratory's Advanced Plant Phenotyping Laboratory (APPL), automated stations image hundreds of plants daily across multiple remote sensing modalities; yet, trait extraction and interpretation remain manual, expert-bound, and strictly post-hoc, making analysis, not acquisition, the binding constraint on discovery. We present an end-to-end agentic AI framework that turns the facility from a data factory into an interactive autonomous, discovery platform, where scientists partner with AI agents to accelerate time to insight. A conversational Co-Scientist Agent translates a scientist's natural-language question into a structured analysis plan, and a headless Compute Agent dispatches Vision Transformer segmentation and trait extraction on the Frontier exascale supercomputer. The two agents run in separate security and resource domains and communicate over a secure, token-authenticated streaming channel, a design that accounts for the federation, data-movement, and provenance realities cloud-native agentic frameworks ignore, ensuring end-to-end provenance is captured for every interaction. The framework turns a days- to weeks-long analysis process into an interactive loop where agents reason over results, recommend next analyses, and respond to follow-up questions in seconds.

</details>


### [273] [Harnessing Textual Refusal Directions for Multimodal Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31876)
*Moreno D'Incà,Massimiliano Mancini,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARS方法，利用从LLM骨干网络中提取的文本拒绝方向，无需多模态安全数据即可实现多模态LLM的安全对齐，通过激活重中心化、自适应缩放和最优层选择，在保持实用性的同时提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM的安全对齐需要收集难以获取的不安全多模态数据，而文本拒绝方向是否能在不同模态间泛化尚不清楚。

Method: 提出Modality-Agnostic Refusal Steering (MARS)，包含三个关键组件：通过激活重中心化修正模态错位、在几何定义的信任区域内自适应缩放引导强度、选择最优干预层，仅在第一个生成token处操作。

Result: 在五个SOTA多模态LLM上评估，MARS在安全、实用性和视频越狱基准测试中实现了一致的安全性提升，同时保持实用性。

Conclusion: 安全相关结构在不同模态间是共享的，文本拒绝方向是多模态对齐的强大且未充分探索的基础。

Abstract: To improve safety in Large Language Models (LLMs) we can either perform post-training alignment or exploit refusal directions in the activation space. Both strategies are less feasible in Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) as they require unsafe multimodal data, harder to collect than their unimodal counterpart. In this work, we relax this constraint and investigate whether textual refusal directions, extracted directly from the LLM backbone, generalize across modalities (i.e., image, video). Preliminary findings confirm this ability, though effectiveness is conditioned by layer selection, steering strength, and cross-modal alignment, with the latter causing safe multimodal inputs to be spuriously steered toward refusal. Building on this, we introduce Modality-Agnostic Refusal Steering (MARS), a light-weight training-free approach that injects multimodal safety without the need for multimodal safety data. MARS corrects modality misalignment via activation re-centering, adaptively scales steering strength within a geometrically defined trust region, and selects the optimal intervention layer, operating at the first generated token. Evaluated on five SOTA MLLMs across safety, utility, and video jailbreak benchmarks, MARS achieves consistent safety gains while preserving utility. These results reveal that safety-relevant structure is shared across modalities and that textual refusal directions are a powerful and underexplored foundation for multimodal alignment.

</details>


### [274] [TreeAgent: A Generalizable Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Bias Labeling in Forestry via Compiled Expert Rules and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31976)
*Shiyi Chen,Nicholas Saban,Collin Hargreaves,Huiqi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体系统（MAS），将专家决策树与视觉语言模型（VLM）协同编排，通过解耦声明式决策（D³）框架实现零修改泛化，在树木偏斜分类任务中以更低标注成本超越监督学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家标注成本高、不一致且难以规模化，尤其是在林业遥感中树木高度偏斜分类等需要专业知识的任务上，传统人工标注成为瓶颈。

Method: 构建多智能体系统（MAS），将专家决策树作为结构先验，由VLM在决策树各个节点执行局部语义感知，并通过多智能体投票机制缓解VLM的随机性；提出解耦声明式决策（D³）框架，支持零修改地泛化到不同的专家决策结构。

Result: 在树木偏斜分类测试集上，该方法优于有监督机器学习基线，并显著减少了所需的专家标注工作量。

Conclusion: 将VLM与专家先验进行智能体编排，能够以更低的标注成本复现专家定义的标注流程，同时保持可解释性。

Abstract: Human-labeled data are widely used as reference annotations in ML, despite known variability across annotators in many expert-driven domains. In addition, expert annotation is slow, inconsistent, and remains a major bottleneck for scaling tasks like tree height bias classification in forestry remote sensing. We propose a multi-agent system (MAS) that orchestrates expert decision trees with Vision-Language Models (VLMs), treating the decision tree as a structural prior while VLMs perform localized semantic perception at individual nodes, with multi-agent voting to mitigate VLM stochasticity. We formalize a Decoupled Declarative Decision (D3) Framework that enables zero-modification generalization across diverse expert-defined decision structures. On a tree bias classification testbed, our framework outperforms supervised ML baselines and reduces the amount of expert labeling effort required. These results suggest that agentic orchestration of VLMs with expert priors can reproduce expert-defined labeling procedures at substantially lower annotation cost while maintaining interpretability.

</details>


### [275] [Self-Study Reconsidered: The Hidden Fragility of Learning from Self-Generated QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32002)
*Ekaterina Alimaskina,Denis Shveykin,Gleb Molodtsov,Igor Shalygin,Alexey Kadeishvili,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了合成问答监督中生成步骤并非中性预处理，而是存在两个脆弱阶段的隐式策略：问题生成阶段存在覆盖饱和、聚焦显著跨度、易被局部呈现劫持等问题；回答阶段模型会遵从文本中的指令式段落。通过将问题绑定固定目标并过滤指令式跨度，可将注入服从率从88%降至13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究合成问答监督中生成步骤对训练信号的影响，揭示其作为隐式策略时的脆弱性问题。

Method: 分析问题生成阶段的选择偏差（覆盖饱和、显著跨度聚焦、提示收敛、局部呈现驱动）和回答阶段的指令遵从现象，提出修复方法（固定目标绑定、指令式跨度过滤）。

Result: 问题生成阶段覆盖早期饱和、聚焦显著跨度；回答阶段模型遵从指令式段落，较大模型在任务冲突下遵从更频繁。修复方法将注入服从率从88%降至13%。

Conclusion: 合成问答生成步骤存在系统性偏差，但可通过不改变训练循环的预处理修复显著降低失败模式。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly taught from synthetic question--answer (QA) supervision: a model generates questions about a document, answers them from the same text, and the resulting pairs are used to fine-tune, distill, or compress knowledge into another model. We show that this generation step is not neutral preprocessing. It is an implicit policy that both selects which evidence becomes training signal and decides how that evidence is answered, and it is fragile at both stages. When choosing what to ask, generators do not scan a document uniformly. Coverage saturates early and concentrates on salient spans, diverse prompts converge on the same regions, and what looks question-worthy is driven by local presentation. As a result, salient artifacts such as poorly cleaned markup can hijack question generation across model families and scales. When answering, the model that produces the supervision tends to obey instruction-like passages embedded in the text. This compliance depends on the intent and surface form of the passage rather than its strictness, and is worst under task conflict, where larger models comply more often. These failure modes arise from choices made during QA generation, so they can be reduced without changing the training loop. Tying each question to a fixed target reduces biased selection, and filtering instruction-like spans before answering lowers mean injection compliance from $88\%$ to $13\%$ in our evaluation while retaining nearly all clean text.

</details>


### [276] [PolicyGuard: From Organizational Policies to Neuro-SymbolicCompliance Review Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32004)
*Sameer Malik,Ayush Singh,Amar Prakash Azad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PolicyGuard是一个神经符号框架，将组织政策转化为可执行的审核引擎（包含类型化关系逻辑规则和原子级提取问题），结合LLM进行局部文档解读与符号评估器进行合规判定，使文档合规审核更明确、可维护且可系统测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端LLM提示方法将政策逻辑隐式化，导致合规决策难以检查、更新和测试，缺乏可解释性和可维护性。

Method: PolicyGuard将组织政策转化为由类型化关系逻辑规则和原子级提取问题组成的可执行审核引擎；LLM基于检索到的文档证据回答局部问题；符号评估器应用形式化规则检测不合规情况。

Result: 在基于公司特定NDA合规审查任务上进行了实例化和评估，合同条款需对照组织特定的谈判政策进行检查。

Conclusion: 通过分离政策形式化、局部文档解读和符号合规评估三个环节，PolicyGuard使文档审查更加明确、可维护且可系统测试。

Abstract: Policy-grounded document review requires determining whether a target document complies with organization-specific policies, guidelines, or playbooks. While large language models can assist with policy interpretation and document analysis, end-to-end prompting leaves the applied policy logic implicit, making compliance decisions difficult to inspect, update, and test. We present PolicyGuard, a neuro-symbolic framework for policy-grounded document compliance review. PolicyGuard converts organizational policy guidance into an executable review engine consisting of typed relational logic rules and atom-level extraction questions. During review, LLMs answer these local questions using retrieved document evidence, and a symbolic evaluator applies the formal rules to detect non-compliance. We instantiate and evaluate PolicyGuard on company-specific NDA compliance review, where contract clauses must be checked against organization-specific negotiation policies. By separating policy formalization, local document interpretation, and symbolic compliance evaluation, PolicyGuard makes document review more explicit, maintainable, and systematically testable.

</details>


### [277] [AxDafny: Agentic Verified Code Generation in Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32007)
*Benjamin Breen,Austin Letson,Borja Requena Pozo,Leopoldo Sarra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AxDafny，一种基于验证器引导的修复框架，用于Dafny语言中的智能体代码生成，能够迭代生成可执行代码及证明工件（不变量、断言、终止参数）；同时构建了LCB-Pro-Dafny基准（250道竞赛编程题），实验表明AxDafny在验证成功率上显著超越GPT-5.5基线，并在DafnyBench上达到92.7%，较最强基线提升6.5个百分点；此外发现验证成功与运行时测试表现衡量代码的不同维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成研究多聚焦于运行时测试通过率，缺乏对形式化验证场景（需同时生成可执行代码与证明工件）的探索；Dafny这类需要验证条件的语言对模型推理能力要求更高，亟需专用方法提升验证成功率。

Method: 提出AxDafny框架，采用验证器引导的迭代修复机制：首先生成初始代码及证明结构，随后利用Dafny验证器反馈的错误信息（如不变式违反、断言失败、循环终止问题）逐步修正实现、不变量、断言和终止参数，直至通过完整验证。

Result: 在LCB-Pro-Dafny基准上，AxDafny相比GPT-5.5基线显著提升验证成功率；在DafnyBench上达到92.7%验证成功率，超过此前最强证明提示基线6.5个百分点；并且验证成功与运行时测试通过率呈现低相关性，表明二者衡量不同代码质量维度。

Conclusion: 验证器引导的迭代修复能有效提升形式化验证场景下的代码生成质量；现有以测试通过率为导向的评估体系无法充分反映验证能力，应建立双维度评估标准；AxDafny为Dafny等验证语言的高质量代码生成提供了可行范式。

Abstract: We study agentic code generation in Dafny, where a model must generate both executable code and the proof artifacts for verification. We present AxDafny, a verifier-guided repair framework that iteratively generates implementations, invariants, assertions, and termination arguments. We also introduce LiveCodeBench-Pro-Dafny (LCB-Pro-Dafny), a benchmark of 250 competition-style programming problems translated into Dafny with formal specifications and a verifier-based evaluation harness. On LCB-Pro-Dafny, AxDafny substantially improves verification success over baseline GPT-5.5 performance. On DafnyBench, AxDafny achieves 92.7\% verification success, outperforming the strongest previously reported proof-hint baseline by 6.5 percentage points. Lastly, we show that verification success and runtime test performance measure different aspects of generated code.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [PolyFlow: Continuous Topology Embedding Flow Matching for Artist-style Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30673)
*Chunshi Wang,Haohan Weng,Junliang Ye,Biwen Lei,Yang Li,Zibo Zhao,Zeqiang Lai,Kaiyi Zhang,Yunhan Yang,Zhuo Chen,Chunchao Guo,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: PolyFlow 提出了一种紧凑拓扑嵌入器，将离散网格数据转换为连续逐顶点状态空间，使得基于 Transformer 的流匹配框架能够以完全并行的方式生成高质量网格，在 Toys4K 基准上超越了自回归方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 Transformer 能生成高质量网格，但其顺序解码导致计算开销巨大，比并行生成模型慢数个数量级；而连续扩散/流匹配方法虽支持并行合成，却无法直接处理网格，因为网格连接性是离散的，与标准连续噪声注入和去噪操作不兼容。

Method: 1) 引入紧凑拓扑嵌入器，将离散网格顶点位置和法线投影为连续逐顶点嵌入，原始邻接信息通过时空距离阈值恢复。2) 预训练并冻结该嵌入器后，原始网格可完全转换为连续逐顶点状态空间（统一位置、法线和隐式拓扑属性）。3) 提出 PolyFlow，基于 Transformer 的流匹配框架，以提取的点云特征为条件，实现完全并行的顶点状态去噪。4) 推理时通过 ODE 求解器快速生成，并支持直接指定目标顶点数来控制输出网格分辨率。

Result: 在 Toys4K 基准上的大量评估表明，PolyFlow 在 Chamfer Distance 和 Hausdorff Distance 上均超越了最先进的自回归基线。

Conclusion: 通过将离散网格拓扑嵌入到连续空间，PolyFlow 成功弥合了离散网格与连续扩散/流匹配方法之间的鸿沟，实现了高效并行的网格生成，同时保持了高质量输出和可控分辨率。

Abstract: Autoregressive Transformers dominate high-quality mesh generation by producing artist-worthy topologies, yet their inherent sequential decoding induces substantial computational overhead, falling orders of magnitude slower than parallel generative models. On the other hand, while continuous diffusion and flow-matching methods support efficient parallel synthesis across a variety of domains, they cannot be directly applied to meshes: mesh connectivity is inherently discrete and incompatible with standard continuous noise injection and denoising operations. To resolve this fundamental incompatibility, we introduce a compact topology embedder that projects discrete mesh vertex positions and normals into continuous per-vertex embeddings, where the original discrete adjacency information can be faithfully recovered via spacetime distance thresholding. After pretraining and freezing this embedder, any raw mesh can be fully converted into a continuous per-vertex state space unifying position, normal, and implicit topological attributes. Built upon this novel continuous mesh representation, we present PolyFlow, a Transformer-based flow-matching framework that achieves fully parallel vertex state denoising conditioned on extracted point-cloud features. During inference, our model completes generation rapidly via an ODE solver, and supports explicit, precise control over output mesh resolution by directly specifying the target vertex count. Extensive evaluations on the Toys4K benchmark demonstrate that PolyFlow surpasses state-of-the-art autoregressive baselines in both Chamfer Distance and Hausdorff Distance.

</details>


### [279] [DANTE-W: Diffuse Albedo Neural Texturing in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30677)
*Guangyu Wang,Tianheng Lu,Ruqi Huang,Lu Fang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: DANTE-W是一种神经纹理框架，能从非结构化图像集合中恢复大规模野外场景的高保真漫反射反照率纹理，无缝集成传统3D重建管线，通过视图空间生成式反照率先验和物理原理的神经渲染增强细节，在基准数据集上验证了其提升重打光保真度的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网格纹理技术直接融合多视角图像会混入固有光照和投射阴影，导致重打光时视觉保真度受损，需解决从野生场景图像中恢复去除光照影响的漫反射反照率纹理的问题。

Method: 提出DANTE-W框架，利用视图空间生成式反照率先验，通过表达性神经表示融合到连贯纹理空间中，并结合基于物理原理的神经渲染增强细粒度纹理细节。

Result: 在包含真实野外场景和合成物体的多样化细粒度纹理基准数据集上，验证了该方法在重建准确反照率纹理和提升重打光保真度方面的有效性。

Conclusion: DANTE-W能够实现高保真漫反射反照率纹理恢复，与传统3D重建管线兼容，显著提升重打光视觉质量。

Abstract: Classical mesh texturing techniques blend captured multi-view images directly, which inevitably suffer from baked-in shading and casted shadows that compromise visual fidelity during relighting. To circumvent this issue, we present a neural texturing framework, namely DANTE-W, to enable high-fidelity diffuse albedo texture recovery from unstructured image collections for large-scale, in-the-wild scenes, which integrates seamlessly with traditional 3D reconstruction pipelines. Given a reconstructed mesh and its surface parameterization, our method fuses view-space generative albedo priors into a coherent texture space via an expressive neural representation, while substantially enhancing fine-grained textural details through physically principled neural rendering. To comprehensively evaluate our method, we curate a benchmark dataset featuring diverse, fine-grained textures, comprising both real-world in-the-wild scenes and synthetic objects. Extensive experiments verify the effectiveness of our approach in reconstructing accurate albedo textures and boosting relighting fidelity. Project page: dante-wild.github.io.

</details>


### [280] [Editable Physically-based Reflections in Raytraced Gaussian Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30861)
*Yohan Poirier-Ginter,Jeffrey Hu,Jean-François Lalonde,George Drettakis*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于3D高斯泼溅的可编辑镜面反射场景重建方法，通过结合学习到的漫反射/镜面反射缓冲、路径追踪和专用训练策略，实现了对反射体和被反射物体的正确重建与实时编辑，支持多弹射反射、粗糙度调整等操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辐射场方法（如3D高斯泼溅）虽能实时渲染场景并高质量重建镜面反射，但通常用反射体后的"虚假"几何体来模拟反射，无法正确分离反射体与被反射物体，导致镜面反射不可编辑。本文目标是正确重建反射体与被反射物体，使得镜面反射可编辑。

Method: 1）利用输入训练视图的漫反射和镜面反射缓冲，优化场景的漫反射版本，并使用路径追踪高效生成基于物理的镜面反射；2）提出专用训练方法确保收敛；3）提出面向3D高斯基元的快速光线追踪算法，支持高效的多弹射反射。

Result: 方法重建了反射体和被反射物体（包括输入图像中不可见的部分）的统一场景表示，支持实时、一致的镜面反射编辑（包括多弹射反射、粗糙度变化等）。主要在合成场景中使用真实缓冲验证，在真实场景中使用学习到的缓冲获得初步结果。

Conclusion: 该工作作为概念验证，展示了利用学习到的缓冲实现可编辑镜面反射的可行性，为场景编辑提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Radiance fields such as 3D Gaussian Splatting allow real-time rendering of scenes captured from photos. They also reconstruct most specular reflections with high visual quality, but typically model them with "fake" reflected geometry, using primitives behind the reflector. Our goal is to correctly reconstruct the reflector and the reflected objects such as to make specular reflections editable. We present a proof of concept which exploits promising learning-based methods to extract diffuse and specular buffers from photos, as well as geometry and BRDF buffers. Our method builds on three key components. First, by using diffuse and specular buffers of input training views, we optimize a diffuse version of the scene and use path tracing to efficiently generate physically based specular reflections. Second, we present a specialized training method that allows this process to converge. Finally, we present a fast ray tracing algorithm for 3D Gaussian primitives that enables efficient multi-bounce reflections. Our method reconstructs reflectors and reflected objects, including those not seen in the input images, in a unique scene representation. Our solution allows real-time, consistent editing of captured scenes with specular reflections, including multi-bounce effects, changing roughness, and more. We mainly show results using ground truth buffers from synthetic scenes, and also preliminary results in real scenes with currently imperfect learning-based buffers. Code and data are available at: https://repo-sam.inria.fr/nerphys/editable-gaussian-reflections/

</details>


### [281] [GRay: Ray Tracing 3D Gaussians Near the Speed of Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30869)
*Yohan Poirier-Ginter,Jean-François Lalonde,George Drettakis*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: GRay是一种针对3D高斯表示的新型快速光线追踪器，利用光线追踪仅评估被射线相交的高斯体的特性，在密集小高斯体场景中实现对数级缩放，相比3DGRT渲染快近4倍、优化快近10倍，同时保持相似质量，并与3DGS速度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯光线追踪(3DGRT)相比3D高斯光栅化(3DGS)速度慢近一个数量级，作者希望弥合这一性能差距，使得光线追踪方法的速度能与光栅化方法相匹配。

Method: 利用光线追踪与光栅化的算法差异：光线追踪仅评估实际被射线相交的高斯体，在密集小高斯体场景中实现对数级缩放（而非线性缩放），并采用密集初始化策略（创建大量小高斯体）来加速光线追踪而非减慢其速度。

Result: GRay渲染速度近4倍于3DGRT，优化速度近10倍于3DGRT，质量相当；与3DGS相比速度具有竞争力，但质量略低。

Conclusion: 密集小高斯体配置此前被忽视，但对光线追踪有利；GRay有效缩小了3D高斯光线追踪与光栅化之间的性能差距。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a popular representation for radiance field reconstruction, distinguished by the rendering speed of its rasterization-based renderer. While 3D Gaussians can also be ray traced, this approach has so far been slower, with 3D Gaussian Ray Tracing (3DGRT) taking nearly one order of magnitude longer to optimize. To address this, we present GRay, a fast ray tracer for 3D Gaussians designed to close this performance gap and match 3DGS's speed. Our method leverages the algorithmic difference between both approaches: unlike rasterization, ray tracing evaluates only Gaussians that are actually intersected by a ray, leading to potentially logarithmic--rather than linear--scaling in the number of primitives. This property allows ray tracing to better exploit dense scenes composed of numerous tiny Gaussians, a configuration which has largely been overlooked. Notably, we show that dense initialization--which creates many small Gaussians--slows down rasterization, but instead speeds up ray tracing. Designed to leverage this effect, GRay renders nearly 4x faster and optimizes nearly 10x faster than 3DGRT while maintaining similar quality, and has competitive speed with 3DGS albeit at somewhat lower quality. Code is available at https://repo-sam.inria.fr/nerphys/gray.

</details>


### [282] [LLM-Driven Personalities for Decision Making in Emergency Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31038)
*Stefano Calzolari,Rubens Montanha,Gabriel Schneider,Gustavo Wide,Paulo Knob,Francesco Strada,Andrea Bottino,Soraia Raupp Musse*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文研究使用大语言模型（LLM）驱动虚拟人在模拟疏散场景中的决策过程，并融入OCEAN人格特质，通过语言提示影响个体行为和群体模拟结果，证明LLM驱动的人格特征能够显著影响智能体决策，产生不同行为模式，增强模拟环境的真实性和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟人需要具备能动性和空间感知能力才能显得可信，而有效的决策是塑造智能体行为的关键。随着AI的快速发展，LLM被探索用于支持决策过程。本文旨在研究如何利用LLM驱动虚拟人在模拟疏散场景中的决策，并通过融入人格特质来提升行为的真实性和多样性。

Method: 在模拟疏散场景中，使用LLM驱动虚拟人的决策过程，并将OCEAN人格特质通过语言提示（prompting）融入智能体表征中，评估人格特质如何通过基于语言的提示影响个体行为和集体模拟结果。

Result: LLM驱动的人格特征显著影响智能体的决策，不同人格特质导致不同的行为模式。由LLM引导的异构人群能够增强模拟环境的真实性和多样性。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的异构人群模拟比传统的基于规则的方法更具灵活性和真实性，能够有效提升虚拟人模拟中行为的多样性和可信度。

Abstract: For virtual humans to appear believable, they must exhibit agency and spatial awareness while interacting with their environment in ways that reflect competence and intelligence. At the core of these capabilities lies effective decision-making, which strongly shapes agent behavior. With the rapid advancement of artificial intelligence, Large Language Models (LLMs) have increasingly been explored as a mechanism to support such decision-making processes. In this work, we investigate the use of LLMs to drive decision-making in virtual humans within a simulated evacuation scenario, incorporating OCEAN personality traits into agent representations. Our goal is to evaluate how personality, expressed through language-based prompts, influences both individual behaviors and collective simulation outcomes. Our results demonstrate that LLM-driven personality profiles significantly impact agents' decisions, leading to distinct behavioral patterns across different traits. These findings suggest that heterogeneous crowds composed of LLM-guided agents can enhance the realism and variability of simulated environments, offering a flexible alternative to traditional rule-based approaches.

</details>


### [283] [DualBrep: A Dual-Field Continuous Representation for B-rep Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31579)
*Yilin Liu,Pradeep Jayaraman,Chinthala Reddy,Xiang Xu,Hooman Shayani*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出DualBrep，一种将B-rep几何与拓扑统一到连续欧几里得域的新型表示方法。通过双标量场（SDF表示几何，UDF通过Voronoi划分隐式编码拓扑）并压缩到共享潜空间，结合流匹配模型联合采样几何与拓扑，最后用神经重建器从连续场中提取显式B-rep模型，在点云逆向工程和生成建模中取得强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-rep的异构结构（连续参数几何+离散拓扑图）给深度学习带来根本性挑战：现有方法通过固定大小填充或序列化tokenization处理可变基元数量，难以应对CAD模型的组合复杂性；且图数据的离散不可微性质阻碍了几何与水密性的端到端优化。

Method: 提出DualBrep连续表示，包含：(1) 双标量场——SDF表示全局形状几何，UDF通过表面元素的Voronoi划分隐式编码拓扑结构；(2) 将双场压缩到单一潜空间实现联合生成；(3) 流匹配模型从单代码联合采样几何与拓扑；(4) 神经重建器从连续双场中提取显式B-rep模型（含棱柱和自由形式基元）。

Result: 在点云逆向工程和基于潜流匹配的生成建模中取得了强性能，证明DualBrep是CAD学习的稳健骨干。

Conclusion: DualBrep通过连续域统一表示解决了B-rep异构结构带来的学习困难，避免了序列化预测的误差累积，为CAD深度学习提供了可端到端优化的新范式。

Abstract: Boundary Representation (B-rep) is the most commonly used data format in Computer-Aided Design (CAD) due to its analytical precision and direct support for parametric editing. However, its heterogeneous structure--continuous parametric geometry combined with discrete topological graphs--poses fundamental challenges for deep learning. Existing methods often predict the heterogeneous B-rep graph directly, using fixed-size padding or sequential tokenization to handle varying primitive counts. These approaches struggle with the combinatorial complexity of CAD models. Furthermore, the discrete, non-differentiable nature of graph data prevents end-to-end optimization of geometry and watertightness. In this work, we introduce DualBrep, a novel continuous representation that unifies B-rep geometry and topology within a fully structured Euclidean domain. DualBrep encodes a CAD model using dual scalar fields: a Signed Distance Function (SDF) representing global shape geometry, and an Unsigned Distance Field (UDF) implicitly encoding topological structure via a Voronoi partitioning of surface elements. Rather than processing these fields independently, we compress them into a single latent space. While the dual-field formulation alone provides a flexible, primitive-free segmentation signal that adapts to arbitrary face counts and surface types, the shared latent makes generation tractable. A Flow Matching model can sample geometry and topology jointly from a single code, avoiding the error accumulation that plagues sequential B-rep predictors. Finally, a neural rebuilder extracts explicit B-rep models--comprising both prismatic and free-form primitives--directly from our continuous dual fields. We demonstrate that DualBrep is a robust backbone for CAD learning, achieving strong performance in point cloud reverse engineering and generative modeling via latent flow matching.

</details>


### [284] [Intrinsic decomposition and editing of 3D Gaussian splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31637)
*Alexandre Lanvin,Jeffrey Hu,Simon Lucas,Adrien Bousseau,George Drettakis*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出将本征分解扩展到高斯泼溅辐射场，通过独立高斯基元集合、数据驱动优化引导和单图像纹理编辑工作流，实现场景的漫反射反照率、 shading 和视角依赖残差分解，支持任意视角下的材质编辑与重渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本征分解在图像编辑中历史悠久，但现有方法局限于2D图像，缺乏对3D辐射场（特别是高斯泼溅表示）的本征分解能力，无法在三维空间中实现材质与光照的分离编辑。

Method: 1) 将本征分解建模为独立的高斯基元集合，各集合自适应对应层特征；2) 利用数据驱动预测引导的多视图照片优化过程，解缠本征分量；3) 提供用户在单张图像上修改平面纹理，通过本征辐射场捕获编辑并重渲染。

Result: 实现了高斯泼溅辐射场的本征分解，支持用户在单张图像上修改平面反照率，即可在任意视角下以合理光照重渲染编辑后的场景。

Conclusion: 成功将本征分解从2D图像扩展到3D高斯泼溅辐射场，为三维场景的材质编辑提供了实用的解耦表示和编辑工作流。

Abstract: Intrinsic decomposition which expresses image colors as the product of diffuse albedo and shading, possibly augmented with view-dependent residuals has a long history in image editing as it enables the modification of object colors and textures without altering lighting. We extend intrinsic decomposition to radiance fields represented with Gaussian splatting by proposing solutions to three key aspects of such decomposition. First, we describe how to model the intrinsic decomposition as independent sets of Gaussian primitives, which allows each set to adapt to the characteristics of the layer it represents. Second, we present an optimization procedure guided by data-driven predictions to disentangle multi-view photographs of a scene into the aforementioned intrinsic sets. Finally, we provide an editing workflow where users modify the texture of planar surfaces simply by modifying the albedo of that surface in one image. Capturing this edit within the intrinsic radiance field allows re-rendering of the edited scene with plausible lighting under arbitrary viewpoints.

</details>


### [285] [Practical High-Fidelity Novel-View Synthesis of Mounted Lepidoptera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31679)
*Kristof Overdulve,Lode Jorissen,Nick Michiels*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端流水线，结合手持对焦堆叠、非接触镜面系统和镜像感知的3D高斯泼溅技术，可将带针标本蝴蝶转化为从任意视角观看的逼真3D模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蝴蝶标本小而脆弱，其微观毛发和翅脉结构需要微距镜头拍摄，但微距镜头景深极浅；同时相机无法置于标本下方拍摄腹面，导致传统方法难以获得高质量的3D数字化模型。

Method: (1) 手持对焦堆叠实现无需三脚架的全程清晰微距拍摄；(2) 非接触第一表面镜面系统反射标本腹面，无需触碰标本；(3) 无分割、镜像感知的3D高斯泼溅扩展，将多视角2D图像重建为3D模型。

Result: 在四个不同标本上验证了该方法能够生成逼真的、可从任意方向观察的3D模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的流水线有效解决了蝴蝶标本3D数字化中景深受限和腹面不可见两大核心难题，实现了高保真重建。

Abstract: Mounted butterflies are among the most striking objects in natural history collections. However, their beauty is notoriously hard to digitize in 3D: they are small and fragile, with microscopic hairs and vein structures. Capturing them in sufficient detail, therefore, requires a macro lens, which has a very limited Depth of Field (DoF). Moreover, a camera body cannot be maneuvered beneath a pinned specimen to photograph its ventral surface (the underside of the wings). We introduce an end-to-end pipeline that resolves these challenges to turn such specimens into photo-realistic 3D models viewable from every direction. It combines three ingredients: handheld focus stacking for all-in-focus macro capture without a tripod, a non-contact first-surface mirror system that exposes the ventral surface without touching the specimen, and a segmentation-free, mirror-aware 3D Gaussian Splatting extension. We validate the reconstructions on four diverse specimens.

</details>


### [286] [NURBS Splatting: A Unified Differentiable Rendering Framework for Vector Graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31764)
*Jingye Qiu,Shizhe Zhou*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出NURBS Splatting，一种将平面有理样条表示为连续高斯场进行可微渲染的统一框架，通过沿曲线参数域和闭合区域内部采样高斯函数，将渲染重构为具有稳定梯度的平滑累积过程，在书法重建、矢量化框架和长样条图像抽象等任务中展现出优于现有方法的稳定性和重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面有理样条在矢量图形和设计中广泛应用，但现有可微渲染方法主要聚焦于贝塞尔曲线，依赖解析光栅化，存在梯度不稳定和灵活性受限的问题。

Method: 将平面有理曲线表示为连续高斯场，沿曲线参数域和闭合区域内部采样高斯函数，将渲染过程重构为具有稳定梯度的平滑累积过程，天然支持长样条、有理权重、非均匀节点和闭合区域填充。

Result: 在书法重建、矢量化框架和长样条图像抽象等任务中，展现出比现有方法更优的稳定性和重建质量。

Conclusion: NURBS Splatting作为统一框架，有效解决了现有可微矢量渲染器在有理样条上的梯度不稳定和灵活性不足问题，验证了其在实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: Differentiable rendering of planar rational splines remains largely underexplored, despite their widespread use in vector graphics and design. Existing differentiable vector renderers primarily focus on Bézier curves and rely on analytic rasterization, which can suffer from gradient instability and limited flexibility. We propose NURBS Splatting, a unified framework that represents planar rational curves as continuous Gaussian fields. By sampling Gaussians along the curve parameter domain and inside closed regions, rendering is reformulated as a smooth accumulation process with stable gradients. Our method naturally supports long splines, rational weights, non-uniform knots, and closed-region filling. We demonstrate its effectiveness in calligraphy reconstruction, vectorization frameworks, and long-spline image abstraction, showing improved stability and reconstruction quality over existing approaches.

</details>
